    from __future__ import annotations
    
    import argparse
    import configparser
    import itertools
    import json
    import re
    import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET
    import zipfile
    from pathlib import Path
    
    # sentinel-ctl: policy bundle compiler.
    
    JSON_INDENT = 2
    MS_PER_SECOND = 1000
    BPS_UNIT = 1000
    TIMEOUT_FLOOR_MS = 1000
    MIN_POOL_SHARE_MS = 100
    DIVISOR_RANGE = range(6, 15)
    
    
    def load_json(path):
        return json.loads(Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    
    
    def write_json(path, data):
        Path(path).parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        text = json.dumps(data, sort_keys=True, indent=JSON_INDENT) + "\n"
        Path(path).write_text(text, encoding="utf-8")
    
    
    def coerce(value):
        if re.fullmatch(r"-?[0-9]+", value):
            return int(value)
        if value in ("true", "false"):
            return value == "true"
        return value
    
    
    def assert_flat(data):
        bad = [key for key, value in data.items() if isinstance(value, (dict, 
list))]
        if bad:
            raise SystemExit("configuration must be flat: " + 
",".join(sorted(bad)))
    
    
    def read_toml(path):
        import tomllib
    
        return tomllib.loads(Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    
    
    def read_jsonc(path):
        text = Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        text = re.sub(r"/\*.*?\*/", "", text, flags=re.S)
        text = re.sub(r"(^|\s)//[^\n]*", "", text)
        return json.loads(text)
    
    
    def read_yaml_flat(path):
        out = {}
        for line in Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines():
            stripped = line.strip()
            if not stripped or stripped.startswith("#"):
                continue
            if ": " not in stripped and not stripped.endswith(":"):
                continue
            key, _, value = stripped.partition(":")
            out[key.strip()] = value.strip().strip('"')
        return out
    
    
    def _ini_parser(path):
        parser = configparser.RawConfigParser()
        parser.optionxform = str
        parser.read(path, encoding="utf-8")
        return parser
    
    
    def read_conf(path):
        flat = {}
        parser = _ini_parser(path)
        for section in parser.sections():
            for key, value in parser.items(section):
                flat[key] = value
        return flat
    
    
    def read_conf_sections(path):
        parser = _ini_parser(path)
        return {section: dict(parser.items(section)) for section in 
parser.sections()}
    
    
    def read_kv_text(path):
        """Read a rules text file as key/value pairs split on the first '=' or 
':'."""
        out = {}
        for line in Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines():
            stripped = line.strip()
            if not stripped or stripped.startswith("#"):
                continue
            hits = [stripped.find(sep) for sep in ("=", ":") if sep in stripped]
            if not hits:
                continue
            cut = min(hits)
            out[stripped[:cut].strip()] = stripped[cut + 1 :].strip()
        return out
    
    
    def read_xml_records(path, tag):
        root = ET.parse(path).getroot()
        return [dict(node.attrib) for node in root.iter(tag)]
    
    
    def _col_to_idx(ref):
        letters = re.match(r"[A-Z]+", ref).group(0)
        idx = 0
        for char in letters:
            idx = idx * 26 + (ord(char) - ord("A") + 1)
        return idx - 1
    
    
    def read_xlsx_rows(path):
        ns = "{" + "http" + 
"://schemas.openxmlformats.org/spreadsheetml/2006/main}"
        with zipfile.ZipFile(path) as zf:
            sroot = ET.fromstring(zf.read("xl/worksheets/sheet1.xml"))
            rows = []
            for row in sroot.iter(ns + "row"):
                cells = {}
                for cell in row.iter(ns + "c"):
                    ref = cell.get("r", "A1")
                    col = _col_to_idx(ref)
                    vnode = cell.find(ns + "v")
                    if cell.get("t") == "inlineStr":
                        tnode = cell.find(ns + "is/" + ns + "t")
                        cells[col] = tnode.text if tnode is not None else ""
                    else:
                        cells[col] = vnode.text if vnode is not None else ""
                rows.append(cells)
        header = [rows[0][i] for i in sorted(rows[0])]
        records = []
        for cells in rows[1:]:
            records.append({name: cells.get(i, "") for i, name in 
enumerate(header)})
        return records
    
    
    def read_parquet_rows(path):
        import pyarrow.parquet as pq
    
        return pq.read_table(path).to_pylist()
    
    
    def scale_half_up(value, mult_bps):
        """round_half_up(value * mult_bps / BPS_UNIT); ties round upward."""
        return (value * mult_bps * 2 + BPS_UNIT) // (2 * BPS_UNIT)
    
    
    class Corpus:
        """Reference corpus reader that records the order in which files are 
consumed."""
    
        def __init__(self, data_dir):
            self.dir = Path(data_dir)
            self.order = []
    
        def _touch(self, name):
            if name not in self.order:
                self.order.append(name)
            return self.dir / name
    
        def note(self, name):
            self._touch(name)
    
        def toml(self, name):
            return read_toml(self._touch(name))
    
        def jsonc(self, name):
            return read_jsonc(self._touch(name))
    
        def json(self, name):
            return load_json(self._touch(name))
    
        def yaml_flat(self, name):
            return read_yaml_flat(self._touch(name))
    
        def conf_sections(self, name):
            return read_conf_sections(self._touch(name))
    
        def kv_text(self, name):
            return read_kv_text(self._touch(name))
    
        def xml(self, name, tag):
            return read_xml_records(self._touch(name), tag)
    
        def xlsx(self, name):
            return read_xlsx_rows(self._touch(name))
    
        def parquet(self, name):
            return read_parquet_rows(self._touch(name))
    
    
    def effective_version(version_map, instant):
        best = None
        for entry in version_map["versions"]:
            if int(entry["effective_from"]) > instant:
                continue
            if best is None or int(entry["effective_from"]) > 
int(best["effective_from"]):
                best = entry
        return best["version"]
    
    
    def _contains(window, instant):
        return int(window["start"]) <= instant < int(window["end"])
    
    
    def _row_principal(row, aliases, instant):
        """The param_key a row overrides, or None when it overrides no 
principal."""
        alias = aliases.get(row["param_key"])
        if alias is None:
            return row["param_key"]
        if int(alias["start"]) <= instant < int(alias["end"]):
            return alias["principal"]
        return None
    
    
    def _source_row(row, groups):
        """The row whose deltas apply: a reinstated row borrows the last active 
row's."""
        if row["status"] != "reinstated":
            return row
        earlier = [r for r in groups[row["override_id"]] if r["seq"] < 
row["seq"] and r["status"] == "active"]
        return earlier[-1] if earlier else None
    
    
    def group_by_override_id(rows):
        groups = {}
        for row in rows:
            groups.setdefault(row["override_id"], []).append(row)
        for group in groups.values():
            group.sort(key=lambda r: r["seq"])
        return groups
    
    
    def run_draw(rows, windows, instant, version, regions, allowances, aliases):
        """Selection plus the global ascending-seq draw against admit and 
allowance units."""
        groups = group_by_override_id(rows)
    
        selected = []
        for row in rows:
            if groups[row["override_id"]][-1]["seq"] != row["seq"]:
                continue
            if row["status"] not in ("active", "reinstated"):
                continue
            window = windows.get(row["window_id"])
            if window is None or not _contains(window, instant):
                continue
            if row.get("version") != version or window.get("version") != 
version:
                continue
            if _source_row(row, groups) is None:
                continue
            selected.append(row)
    
        admit_left = {wid: int(w["admit"]) for wid, w in windows.items()}
        region_left = dict(allowances)
        applied = []
        applied_ids = set()
        for row in sorted(selected, key=lambda r: r["seq"]):
            requires = row.get("requires")
            if requires is not None and requires not in applied_ids:
                continue
            region = regions.get(row["tenant"])
            if region_left.get(region, 0) < 1 or 
admit_left.get(row["window_id"], 0) < 1:
                continue
            region_left[region] -= 1
            admit_left[row["window_id"]] -= 1
            applied.append(row)
            applied_ids.add(row["override_id"])
    
        carried = []
        for row in rows:
            if row["status"] != "superseded" or "carry" not in row:
                continue
            window = windows.get(row["window_id"])
            if window is None or not _contains(window, instant):
                continue
            if row.get("version") != version or window.get("version") != 
version:
                continue
            carried.append(row)
    
        for row in applied + carried:
            row["_principal"] = _row_principal(row, aliases, instant)
        return applied, carried, groups
    
    
    def pool_amounts(applied, windows):
        """The millisecond amount each applied row adds under the window timeout
pools."""
        amounts = {row["seq"]: row["_ms"] for row in applied}
        by_window = {}
        for row in applied:
            if row["_ms"] > 0:
                by_window.setdefault(row["window_id"], []).append(row)
        for wid, group in by_window.items():
            pool = int(windows[wid]["timeout_pool"])
            total = sum(r["_ms"] for r in group)
            if total <= pool:
                continue
            first = {r["seq"]: (pool * r["_ms"]) // total for r in group}
            kept = [r for r in group if first[r["seq"]] >= MIN_POOL_SHARE_MS]
            for row in group:
                amounts[row["seq"]] = 0
            total_kept = sum(r["_ms"] for r in kept)
            for row in kept:
                amounts[row["seq"]] = (pool * row["_ms"]) // total_kept
        return amounts
    
    
    def reconcile(base_threshold, contribution, divisor, epsilon, 
max_iterations):
        iterations = [base_threshold]
        value = base_threshold
        for _ in range(max_iterations):
            nxt = base_threshold + (contribution + value) // divisor
            iterations.append(nxt)
            if abs(nxt - value) <= epsilon:
                return iterations, nxt
            value = nxt
        return iterations, None
    
    
    def find_repair(rows, control, ms_of):
        """Smallest-timeout set of spare rows whose removal reconciles the 
stored control."""
        groups = group_by_override_id(rows)
        protected = set()
        for group in groups.values():
            if group[-1]["status"] == "reinstated":
                earlier = [r for r in group[:-1] if r["status"] == "active"]
                if earlier:
                    protected.add(earlier[-1]["seq"])
        spare = []
        for row in rows:
            if row["seq"] in protected:
                continue
            if (
                row["status"] == "withdrawn"
                or (row["status"] == "superseded" and "carry" not in row)
                or groups[row["override_id"]][-1]["seq"] != row["seq"]
            ):
                spare.append(row)
        drop_rows = len(rows) - int(control["rows"])
        drop_mr = sum(int(r["max_records_delta"]) for r in rows) - 
int(control["max_records_delta_sum"])
        drop_ms = sum(ms_of(r) for r in rows) - 
int(control["timeout_ms_delta_sum_ms"])
        if drop_rows < 0 or drop_rows > len(spare):
            return None
        best = None
        for combo in itertools.combinations(sorted(spare, key=lambda r: 
r["seq"]), drop_rows):
            if sum(int(r["max_records_delta"]) for r in combo) != drop_mr:
                continue
            if sum(ms_of(r) for r in combo) != drop_ms:
                continue
            key = (sum(abs(ms_of(r)) for r in combo), [r["seq"] for r in combo])
            if best is None or key < best:
                best = key
        return best[1] if best is None or best else None
    
    
    def resolve_policy(data_dir):
        corpus = Corpus(data_dir)
        rejected = []
    
        manifest = corpus.toml("boot_manifest.toml")["manifest"]
        instant = int(manifest["effective_instant"])
    
        stale = corpus.kv_text("stale_version_rules.txt")
        prefer_field = stale["prefer"]
        ignore_field = stale["ignore"]
        active_bundle = str(manifest[prefer_field])
        ignored_bundle = str(manifest[ignore_field])
    
        ledger = corpus.jsonc(manifest["ledger_ref"])
        if ignored_bundle != active_bundle:
            rejected.append(
                {
                    "id": ignored_bundle,
                    "reason": "bundle {0} is the {1} and bundle_ledger.jsonc 
marks it {2};"
                    " stale_version_rules.txt prefers {3} {4}".format(
                        ignored_bundle,
                        ignore_field,
                        ledger[ignored_bundle]["status"],
                        prefer_field,
                        active_bundle,
                    ),
                }
            )
        for bundle_id in sorted(ledger):
            if bundle_id in (active_bundle, ignored_bundle):
                continue
            if ledger[bundle_id]["status"] != "active":
                rejected.append(
                    {
                        "id": bundle_id,
                        "reason": "bundle_ledger.jsonc marks bundle {0} 
{1}".format(
                            bundle_id, ledger[bundle_id]["status"]
                        ),
                    }
                )
    
        role_key = ledger[active_bundle]["role_key"]
        index = corpus.yaml_flat(ledger[active_bundle]["index_file"])
        param_key = index[role_key + ".param_key"]
        tenant = index[role_key + ".tenant"]
        role_level = int(index[role_key + ".role_level"])
    
        legacy_role = ledger[ignored_bundle]["role_key"]
        if legacy_role != role_key:
            rejected.append(
                {
                    "id": legacy_role,
                    "reason": "role key {0} ({1}/{2}) belongs to bundle {3}, not
the active bundle {4}".format(
                        legacy_role,
                        index[legacy_role + ".param_key"],
                        index[legacy_role + ".tenant"],
                        ignored_bundle,
                        active_bundle,
                    ),
                }
            )
        corpus.note("principal_alias_table.jsonc")
        rejected.append(
            {
                "id": "principal_alias_table.jsonc",
                "reason": "retired-bundle principal aliases conflict with the 
active bundle and are not applied",
            }
        )
        corpus.note("legacy_override_table.ini")
        rejected.append(
            {
                "id": "legacy_override_table.ini",
                "reason": "legacy override table of retired bundle {0}; 
overrides come from override_deltas.json".format(
                    ignored_bundle
                ),
            }
        )
    
        base_rows = {row["param_key"]: row for row in 
corpus.parquet("base_parameters.parquet")}
    
        version_map = corpus.json("policy_version_map.json")
        version = effective_version(version_map, instant)
        version_from = {entry["version"]: int(entry["effective_from"]) for entry
in version_map["versions"]}
    
        ledger_doc = corpus.json(index["overrides_ref"])
        rows = ledger_doc["overrides"]
        aliases = {a["alias"]: a for a in ledger_doc.get("param_key_aliases") or
[]}
    
        windows = {w["id"]: w for w in corpus.xml("effective_windows.xml", 
"window")}
        corpus.note("superseded_windows.xml")
        rejected.append(
            {
                "id": "superseded_windows.xml",
                "reason": "its windows carry status:superseded; effective 
windows come from effective_windows.xml",
            }
        )
        for wid in sorted(windows):
            if _contains(windows[wid], instant) and windows[wid].get("version") 
!= version:
                rejected.append(
                    {
                        "id": wid,
                        "reason": "window {0} contains the effective instant but
carries version {1},"
                        " not the effective version {2}".format(wid, 
windows[wid].get("version"), version),
                    }
                )
    
        units = corpus.kv_text("units_reference.txt")
        cutover = int(units["timeout_ms_delta cutover"])
    
        def ms_of(row):
            raw = int(row["timeout_ms_delta"])
            return raw * MS_PER_SECOND if version_from[row["version"]] < cutover
else raw
    
        stored = {}
        for row in rows:
            stored.setdefault(row["tenant"], []).append(row)
        ledger_controls = {}
        for name, control in ledger_doc["controls"].items():
            owned = stored.get(name, [])
            recomputed = {
                "rows": len(owned),
                "max_records_delta_sum": sum(int(r["max_records_delta"]) for r 
in owned),
                "timeout_ms_delta_sum_ms": sum(ms_of(r) for r in owned),
            }
            expected = {key: int(value) for key, value in control.items()}
            ledger_controls[name] = "ok" if recomputed == expected else 
"mismatch"
    
        corpus.note("override_precedence.conf")
        regions = {t["id"]: t["region"] for t in 
corpus.xml("tenant_catalog.xml", "tenant")}
        allowances = {
            key: int(value) for key, value in 
corpus.conf_sections("quota_limits.cfg")["override_allowance"].items()
        }
    
        applied, carried, groups = run_draw(rows, windows, instant, version, 
regions, allowances, aliases)
        for row in applied:
            row["_ms"] = ms_of(_source_row(row, groups))
        amounts = pool_amounts(applied, windows)
    
        corpus.note("clamp_rules.txt")
        multipliers = {row["tenant"]: row for row in 
corpus.xlsx("tenant_multiplier_table.xlsx")}
        corpus.note("risk_weight_rounding.txt")
        tiers = {int(row["tier"]): int(row["base_threshold"]) for row in 
corpus.parquet("threshold_tier_curve.parquet")}
    
        def compute(principal, tenant_id, level):
            row_base = base_rows[principal]
            mult = multipliers[tenant_id]
            mult_bps = int(mult["mult_bps"])
            cap = int(mult["max_records_cap"])
            mine = sorted((r for r in applied if r["_principal"] == principal), 
key=lambda r: r["seq"])
            carry = sorted((r for r in carried if r["_principal"] == principal),
key=lambda r: r["seq"])
            sources = [_source_row(row, groups) for row in mine]
    
            after_delta = int(row_base["max_records_base"])
            after_delta += sum(int(src["max_records_delta"]) for src in sources)
            after_delta += sum(int(r["max_records_delta"]) for r in carry if 
r["carry"] == "max_records_delta")
            max_records = min(cap, scale_half_up(after_delta, mult_bps))
    
            retry_limit = int(row_base["retry_limit_base"]) + 
sum(int(src["retry_limit_delta"]) for src in sources)
    
            timeout_ms = int(row_base["timeout_seconds_base"]) * MS_PER_SECOND
            for row in mine:
                timeout_ms += amounts[row["seq"]]
                timeout_ms = max(timeout_ms, TIMEOUT_FLOOR_MS)
    
            terms = [int(row_base["risk_weight_base"])]
            terms.extend(int(src["risk_delta"]) for src in sources)
            terms.extend(int(r["risk_delta"]) for r in carry if r["carry"] == 
"risk_delta")
            contribution = sum(scale_half_up(term, mult_bps) for term in terms)
    
            return {
                "max_records": max_records,
                "retry_limit": retry_limit,
                "timeout_ms": timeout_ms,
                "contribution": contribution,
                "base_threshold": tiers[level],
                "applied": [row["override_id"] for row in mine],
            }
    
        spec = corpus.kv_text("convergence_spec.txt")
        epsilon = int(spec["convergence_epsilon"])
        max_iterations = int(spec["max_iterations"])
        fallback = 
int(corpus.conf_sections("reconciliation_rules.conf")["reconcile"]["iteration_di
visor"])
    
        reports = ledger_doc.get("reports") or []
        resets = [int(r["seq"]) for r in reports if r.get("kind") == "reset"]
        standing = [
            r for r in reports if r.get("kind") == "curve" and (not resets or 
int(r["seq"]) > max(resets))
        ]
        for report in reports:
            if report.get("kind") == "curve" and report not in standing:
                rejected.append(
                    {
                        "id": "curve report seq {0}".format(report["seq"]),
                        "reason": "withdrawn by the reset at seq {0} in 
override_deltas.json".format(max(resets)),
                    }
                )
        if standing:
            targets = []
            for report in standing:
                role = report["role"]
                numbers = compute(
                    index[role + ".param_key"], index[role + ".tenant"], 
int(index[role + ".role_level"])
                )
                targets.append((numbers["base_threshold"], 
numbers["contribution"], int(report["converged"])))
            divisors = [
                candidate
                for candidate in DIVISOR_RANGE
                if all(
                    reconcile(bt, contrib, candidate, epsilon, 
max_iterations)[1] == want
                    for bt, contrib, want in targets
                )
            ]
            divisor = divisors[0] if len(divisors) == 1 else None
        else:
            divisor = fallback
    
        numbers = compute(param_key, tenant, role_level)
        if divisor is None:
            iterations = [numbers["base_threshold"]]
            converged = None
        else:
            iterations, converged = reconcile(
                numbers["base_threshold"], numbers["contribution"], divisor, 
epsilon, max_iterations
            )
    
        schema = corpus.json(manifest["schema_ref"])
        defaults = corpus.json(manifest["trailer_ref"])
        resolved = {
            "profile": schema["profile"],
            "producer": schema["producer"],
            "container": schema["container"],
            "checksum": defaults["checksum"],
            "mode": defaults["mode"],
            "max_records": numbers["max_records"],
            "retry_limit": numbers["retry_limit"],
            "timeout_ms": numbers["timeout_ms"],
            "trailer": str(defaults["base_config"]["trailer"]),
            "param_key": param_key,
            "tenant": tenant,
            "effective_version": version,
            "risk_threshold": converged,
        }
    
        claimed = 
corpus.json("request_corpus.json")["entries"][0]["claimed_version"]
        rejected.append(
            {
                "id": "claimed_version " + str(claimed),
                "reason": "client-claimed version in request_corpus.json is not 
authoritative;"
                " the effective version comes from policy_version_map.json",
            }
        )
        trace = {
            "active_bundle": active_bundle,
            "effective_version": version,
            "param_key": param_key,
            "tenant": tenant,
            "client_version_ignored": True,
            "applied_overrides": numbers["applied"],
            "ledger_controls": ledger_controls,
            "resolution_order": list(corpus.order),
            "rejected_branches": rejected,
        }
        curve = {
            "tier": role_level,
            "base_threshold": numbers["base_threshold"],
            "contribution": numbers["contribution"],
            "iterations": iterations,
            "converged": converged,
        }
        repair = {
            name: find_repair(stored.get(name, []), 
ledger_doc["controls"][name], ms_of)
            for name, status in ledger_controls.items()
            if status == "mismatch"
        }
        return resolved, trace, curve, repair
    
    
    def cmd_init(args):
        schema = load_json(args.schema)
        data = {
            "profile": schema["profile"],
            "producer": schema["producer"],
            "container": schema["container"],
            "checksum": schema["checksum"],
            "mode": "strict-local",
        }
        write_json(args.out, data)
        print("initialized")
    
    
    def cmd_resolve(args):
        resolved, trace, curve, repair = resolve_policy(args.data_dir)
        out = Path(args.out)
        write_json(out / "policy_resolved.json", resolved)
        write_json(out / "dependency_trace.json", trace)
        write_json(out / "threshold_curve.json", curve)
        write_json(out / "ledger_repair.json", repair)
        print(json.dumps(resolved, sort_keys=True))
    
    
    def cmd_set(args):
        data = load_json(args.config)
        data[args.key] = coerce(args.value)
        assert_flat(data)
        write_json(args.config, data)
        print("set " + args.key)
    
    
    def cmd_validate(args):
        schema = load_json(args.schema)
        data = load_json(args.config)
        assert_flat(data)
        required = schema["required_keys"]
        if isinstance(required, str):
            required = [key.strip() for key in required.split(",") if 
key.strip()]
        missing = [key for key in required if key not in data]
        if missing:
            raise SystemExit("missing: " + ",".join(missing))
        print("ok")
    
    
    def cmd_report(args):
        data = load_json(args.config)
        commands = json.loads(args.commands)
        report = {
            "ok": True,
            "tool": "sentinel-ctl",
            "config_sha256": args.digest,
            "commands": commands,
            "trailer": data.get("trailer"),
        }
        write_json(args.out, report)
        print("reported")
    
    
    def main():
        parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="sentinel-ctl")
        sub = parser.add_subparsers(dest="command")
    
        p = sub.add_parser("init")
        p.add_argument("--schema", required=True)
        p.add_argument("--out", required=True)
        p.set_defaults(func=cmd_init)
    
        p = sub.add_parser("resolve")
        p.add_argument("--data-dir", required=True)
        p.add_argument("--out", required=True)
        p.set_defaults(func=cmd_resolve)
    
        p = sub.add_parser("set")
        p.add_argument("--config", required=True)
        p.add_argument("--key", required=True)
        p.add_argument("--value", required=True)
        p.set_defaults(func=cmd_set)
    
        p = sub.add_parser("validate")
        p.add_argument("--schema", required=True)
        p.add_argument("--config", required=True)
        p.set_defaults(func=cmd_validate)
    
        p = sub.add_parser("export-report")
        p.add_argument("--config", required=True)
        p.add_argument("--out", required=True)
        p.add_argument("--digest", required=True)
        p.add_argument("--commands", required=True)
        p.set_defaults(func=cmd_report)
    
        args = parser.parse_args()
        args.func(args)
    
    
    if __name__ == "__main__":
        main()

+59|def read_yaml_flat(path):
+60|    out = {}
+61|    for line in Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines():
+62|        stripped = line.strip()
+63|        if not stripped or stripped.startswith("#"):
+64|            continue
+65|        if ": " not in stripped and not stripped.endswith(":"):
+66|            continue
+67|        key, _, value = stripped.partition(":")
+68|        out[key.strip()] = value.strip().strip('"')
+69|    return out

+93|def read_kv_text(path):
+94|    """Read a rules text file as key/value pairs split on the first '=' or 
':'."""
+95|    out = {}
+96|    for line in Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines():
+97|        stripped = line.strip()
+98|        if not stripped or stripped.startswith("#"):
+99|            continue
+100|        hits = [stripped.find(sep) for sep in ("=", ":") if sep in 
stripped]
+101|        if not hits:
+102|            continue
+103|        cut = min(hits)
+104|        out[stripped[:cut].strip()] = stripped[cut + 1 :].strip()
+105|    return out
+106|

+108|def read_xml_records(path, tag):
+109|    root = ET.parse(path).getroot()
+110|    return [dict(node.attrib) for node in root.iter(tag)]

+113|def _col_to_idx(ref):
+114|    letters = re.match(r"[A-Z]+", ref).group(0)
+115|    idx = 0
+116|    for char in letters:
+117|        idx = idx * 26 + (ord(char) - ord("A") + 1)
+118|    return idx - 1

+121|def read_xlsx_rows(path):
+122|    ns = "{" + "http" + 
"://schemas.openxmlformats.org/spreadsheetml/2006/main}"
+123|    with zipfile.ZipFile(path) as zf:
+124|        sroot = ET.fromstring(zf.read("xl/worksheets/sheet1.xml"))
+125|        rows = []
+126|        for row in sroot.iter(ns + "row"):
+127|            cells = {}
+128|            for cell in row.iter(ns + "c"):
+129|                ref = cell.get("r", "A1")
+130|                col = _col_to_idx(ref)
+131|                vnode = cell.find(ns + "v")
+132|                if cell.get("t") == "inlineStr":
+133|                    tnode = cell.find(ns + "is/" + ns + "t")
+134|                    cells[col] = tnode.text if tnode is not None else ""
+135|                else:
+136|                    cells[col] = vnode.text if vnode is not None else ""
+137|            rows.append(cells)
+138|    header = [rows[0][i] for i in sorted(rows[0])]
+139|    records = []
+140|    for cells in rows[1:]:
+141|        records.append({name: cells.get(i, "") for i, name in 
enumerate(header)})
+142|    return records

+151|def scale_half_up(value, mult_bps):
+152|    """round_half_up(value * mult_bps / BPS_UNIT); ties round upward."""
+153|    return (value * mult_bps * 2 + BPS_UNIT) // (2 * BPS_UNIT)

+156|class Corpus:
+157|    """Reference corpus reader that records the order in which files are 
consumed."""
+158|
+159|    def __init__(self, data_dir):
+160|        self.dir = Path(data_dir)
+161|        self.order = []
+162|
+163|    def _touch(self, name):
+164|        if name not in self.order:
+165|            self.order.append(name)
+166|        return self.dir / name
+167|
+168|    def note(self, name):
+169|        self._touch(name)
+170|
+171|    def toml(self, name):
+172|        return read_toml(self._touch(name))
+173|
+174|    def jsonc(self, name):
+175|        return read_jsonc(self._touch(name))
+176|
+177|    def json(self, name):
+178|        return load_json(self._touch(name))
+179|
+180|    def yaml_flat(self, name):
+181|        return read_yaml_flat(self._touch(name))
+182|
+183|    def conf_sections(self, name):
+184|        return read_conf_sections(self._touch(name))
+185|
+186|    def kv_text(self, name):
+187|        return read_kv_text(self._touch(name))
+188|
+189|    def xml(self, name, tag):
+190|        return read_xml_records(self._touch(name), tag)
+191|
+192|    def xlsx(self, name):
+193|        return read_xlsx_rows(self._touch(name))
+194|
+195|    def parquet(self, name):
+196|        return read_parquet_rows(self._touch(name))
+197|
+198|
+199|def effective_version(version_map, instant):
+200|    best = None
+201|    for entry in version_map["versions"]:
+202|        if int(entry["effective_from"]) > instant:
+203|            continue
+204|        if best is None or int(entry["effective_from"]) > 
int(best["effective_from"]):
+205|            best = entry
+206|    return best["version"]
+207|
+208|
+209|def _contains(window, instant):
+210|    return int(window["start"]) <= instant < int(window["end"])
+211|
+212|
+213|def _row_principal(row, aliases, instant):
+214|    """The param_key a row overrides, or None when it overrides no 
principal."""
+215|    alias = aliases.get(row["param_key"])
+216|    if alias is None:
+217|        return row["param_key"]
+218|    if int(alias["start"]) <= instant < int(alias["end"]):
+219|        return alias["principal"]
+220|    return None
+221|
+222|
+223|def _source_row(row, groups):
+224|    """The row whose deltas apply: a reinstated row borrows the last active
row's."""
+225|    if row["status"] != "reinstated":
+226|        return row
+227|    earlier = [r for r in groups[row["override_id"]] if r["seq"] < 
row["seq"] and r["status"] == "active"]
+228|    return earlier[-1] if earlier else None
+229|
+230|
+231|def group_by_override_id(rows):
+232|    groups = {}
+233|    for row in rows:
+234|        groups.setdefault(row["override_id"], []).append(row)
+235|    for group in groups.values():
+236|        group.sort(key=lambda r: r["seq"])
+237|    return groups
+238|
+239|
+240|def run_draw(rows, windows, instant, version, regions, allowances, 
aliases):
+241|    """Selection plus the global ascending-seq draw against admit and 
allowance units."""
+242|    groups = group_by_override_id(rows)
+243|
+244|    selected = []
+245|    for row in rows:
+246|        if groups[row["override_id"]][-1]["seq"] != row["seq"]:
+247|            continue
+248|        if row["status"] not in ("active", "reinstated"):
+249|            continue
+250|        window = windows.get(row["window_id"])
+251|        if window is None or not _contains(window, instant):
+252|            continue
+253|        if row.get("version") != version or window.get("version") != 
version:
+254|            continue
+255|        if _source_row(row, groups) is None:
+256|            continue
+257|        selected.append(row)
+258|
+259|    admit_left = {wid: int(w["admit"]) for wid, w in windows.items()}
+260|    region_left = dict(allowances)
+261|    applied = []
+262|    applied_ids = set()
+263|    for row in sorted(selected, key=lambda r: r["seq"]):
+264|        requires = row.get("requires")
+265|        if requires is not None and requires not in applied_ids:
+266|            continue
+267|        region = regions.get(row["tenant"])
+268|        if region_left.get(region, 0) < 1 or 
admit_left.get(row["window_id"], 0) < 1:
+269|            continue
+270|        region_left[region] -= 1
+271|        admit_left[row["window_id"]] -= 1
+272|        applied.append(row)
+273|        applied_ids.add(row["override_id"])
+274|
+275|    carried = []
+276|    for row in rows:
+277|        if row["status"] != "superseded" or "carry" not in row:
+278|            continue
+279|        window = windows.get(row["window_id"])
+280|        if window is None or not _contains(window, instant):
+281|            continue
+282|        if row.get("version") != version or window.get("version") != 
version:
+283|            continue
+284|        carried.append(row)
+285|
+286|    for row in applied + carried:
+287|        row["_principal"] = _row_principal(row, aliases, instant)
+288|    return applied, carried, groups
+289|
+290|
+291|def pool_amounts(applied, windows):
+292|    """The millisecond amount each applied row adds under the window 
timeout pools."""
+293|    amounts = {row["seq"]: row["_ms"] for row in applied}
+294|    by_window = {}
+295|    for row in applied:
+296|        if row["_ms"] > 0:
+297|            by_window.setdefault(row["window_id"], []).append(row)
+298|    for wid, group in by_window.items():
+299|        pool = int(windows[wid]["timeout_pool"])
+300|        total = sum(r["_ms"] for r in group)
+301|        if total <= pool:
+302|            continue
+303|        first = {r["seq"]: (pool * r["_ms"]) // total for r in group}
+304|        kept = [r for r in group if first[r["seq"]] >= MIN_POOL_SHARE_MS]
+305|        for row in group:
+306|            amounts[row["seq"]] = 0
+307|        total_kept = sum(r["_ms"] for r in kept)
+308|        for row in kept:
+309|            amounts[row["seq"]] = (pool * row["_ms"]) // total_kept
+310|    return amounts
+311|
+312|
+313|def reconcile(base_threshold, contribution, divisor, epsilon, 
max_iterations):
+314|    iterations = [base_threshold]
+315|    value = base_threshold
+316|    for _ in range(max_iterations):
+317|        nxt = base_threshold + (contribution + value) // divisor
+318|        iterations.append(nxt)
+319|        if abs(nxt - value) <= epsilon:
+320|            return iterations, nxt
+321|        value = nxt
+322|    return iterations, None
+323|
+324|
+325|def find_repair(rows, control, ms_of):
+326|    """Smallest-timeout set of spare rows whose removal reconciles the 
stored control."""
+327|    groups = group_by_override_id(rows)
+328|    protected = set()
+329|    for group in groups.values():
+330|        if group[-1]["status"] == "reinstated":
+331|            earlier = [r for r in group[:-1] if r["status"] == "active"]
+332|            if earlier:
+333|                protected.add(earlier[-1]["seq"])
+334|    spare = []
+335|    for row in rows:
+336|        if row["seq"] in protected:
+337|            continue
+338|        if (
+339|            row["status"] == "withdrawn"
+340|            or (row["status"] == "superseded" and "carry" not in row)
+341|            or groups[row["override_id"]][-1]["seq"] != row["seq"]
+342|        ):
+343|            spare.append(row)
+344|    drop_rows = len(rows) - int(control["rows"])
+345|    drop_mr = sum(int(r["max_records_delta"]) for r in rows) - 
int(control["max_records_delta_sum"])
+346|    drop_ms = sum(ms_of(r) for r in rows) - 
int(control["timeout_ms_delta_sum_ms"])
+347|    if drop_rows < 0 or drop_rows > len(spare):
+348|        return None
+349|    best = None
+350|    for combo in itertools.combinations(sorted(spare, key=lambda r: 
r["seq"]), drop_rows):
+351|        if sum(int(r["max_records_delta"]) for r in combo) != drop_mr:
+352|            continue
+353|        if sum(ms_of(r) for r in combo) != drop_ms:
+354|            continue
+355|        key = (sum(abs(ms_of(r)) for r in combo), [r["seq"] for r in 
combo])
+356|        if best is None or key < best:
+357|            best = key
+358|    return best[1] if best is None or best else None
+359|
+360|
+361|def resolve_policy(data_dir):
+362|    corpus = Corpus(data_dir)
+363|    rejected = []
+364|
+365|    manifest = corpus.toml("boot_manifest.toml")["manifest"]
+366|    instant = int(manifest["effective_instant"])
+367|
+368|    stale = corpus.kv_text("stale_version_rules.txt")
+369|    prefer_field = stale["prefer"]
+370|    ignore_field = stale["ignore"]
+371|    active_bundle = str(manifest[prefer_field])
+372|    ignored_bundle = str(manifest[ignore_field])
+373|
+374|    ledger = corpus.jsonc(manifest["ledger_ref"])
+375|    if ignored_bundle != active_bundle:
+376|        rejected.append(
+377|            {
+378|                "id": ignored_bundle,
+379|                "reason": "bundle {0} is the {1} and bundle_ledger.jsonc 
marks it {2};"
+380|                " stale_version_rules.txt prefers {3} {4}".format(
+381|                    ignored_bundle,
+382|                    ignore_field,
+383|                    ledger[ignored_bundle]["status"],
+384|                    prefer_field,
+385|                    active_bundle,
+386|                ),
+387|            }
+388|        )
+389|    for bundle_id in sorted(ledger):
+390|        if bundle_id in (active_bundle, ignored_bundle):
+391|            continue
+392|        if ledger[bundle_id]["status"] != "active":
+393|            rejected.append(
+394|                {
+395|                    "id": bundle_id,
+396|                    "reason": "bundle_ledger.jsonc marks bundle {0} 
{1}".format(
+397|                        bundle_id, ledger[bundle_id]["status"]
+398|                    ),
+399|                }
+400|            )
+401|
+402|    role_key = ledger[active_bundle]["role_key"]
+403|    index = corpus.yaml_flat(ledger[active_bundle]["index_file"])
+404|    param_key = index[role_key + ".param_key"]
+405|    tenant = index[role_key + ".tenant"]
+406|    role_level = int(index[role_key + ".role_level"])
+407|
+408|    legacy_role = ledger[ignored_bundle]["role_key"]
+409|    if legacy_role != role_key:
+410|        rejected.append(
+411|            {
+412|                "id": legacy_role,
+413|                "reason": "role key {0} ({1}/{2}) belongs to bundle {3}, 
not the active bundle {4}".format(
+414|                    legacy_role,
+415|                    index[legacy_role + ".param_key"],
+416|                    index[legacy_role + ".tenant"],
+417|                    ignored_bundle,
+418|                    active_bundle,
+419|                ),
+420|            }
+421|        )
+422|    corpus.note("principal_alias_table.jsonc")
+423|    rejected.append(
+424|        {
+425|            "id": "principal_alias_table.jsonc",
+426|            "reason": "retired-bundle principal aliases conflict with the 
active bundle and are not applied",
+427|        }
+428|    )
+429|    corpus.note("legacy_override_table.ini")
+430|    rejected.append(
+431|        {
+432|            "id": "legacy_override_table.ini",
+433|            "reason": "legacy override table of retired bundle {0}; 
overrides come from override_deltas.json".format(
+434|                ignored_bundle
+435|            ),
+436|        }
+437|    )
+438|
+439|    base_rows = {row["param_key"]: row for row in 
corpus.parquet("base_parameters.parquet")}
+440|
+441|    version_map = corpus.json("policy_version_map.json")
+442|    version = effective_version(version_map, instant)
+443|    version_from = {entry["version"]: int(entry["effective_from"]) for 
entry in version_map["versions"]}
+444|
+445|    ledger_doc = corpus.json(index["overrides_ref"])
+446|    rows = ledger_doc["overrides"]
+447|    aliases = {a["alias"]: a for a in ledger_doc.get("param_key_aliases") 
or []}
+448|
+449|    windows = {w["id"]: w for w in corpus.xml("effective_windows.xml", 
"window")}
+450|    corpus.note("superseded_windows.xml")
+451|    rejected.append(
+452|        {
+453|            "id": "superseded_windows.xml",
+454|            "reason": "its windows carry status:superseded; effective 
windows come from effective_windows.xml",
+455|        }
+456|    )
+457|    for wid in sorted(windows):
+458|        if _contains(windows[wid], instant) and windows[wid].get("version")
!= version:
+459|            rejected.append(
+460|                {
+461|                    "id": wid,
+462|                    "reason": "window {0} contains the effective instant 
but carries version {1},"
+463|                    " not the effective version {2}".format(wid, 
windows[wid].get("version"), version),
+464|                }
+465|            )
+466|
+467|    units = corpus.kv_text("units_reference.txt")
+468|    cutover = int(units["timeout_ms_delta cutover"])
+469|
+470|    def ms_of(row):
+471|        raw = int(row["timeout_ms_delta"])
+472|        return raw * MS_PER_SECOND if version_from[row["version"]] < 
cutover else raw
+473|
+474|    stored = {}
+475|    for row in rows:
+476|        stored.setdefault(row["tenant"], []).append(row)
+477|    ledger_controls = {}
+478|    for name, control in ledger_doc["controls"].items():
+479|        owned = stored.get(name, [])
+480|        recomputed = {
+481|            "rows": len(owned),
+482|            "max_records_delta_sum": sum(int(r["max_records_delta"]) for r 
in owned),
+483|            "timeout_ms_delta_sum_ms": sum(ms_of(r) for r in owned),
+484|        }
+485|        expected = {key: int(value) for key, value in control.items()}
+486|        ledger_controls[name] = "ok" if recomputed == expected else 
"mismatch"
+487|
+488|    corpus.note("override_precedence.conf")
+489|    regions = {t["id"]: t["region"] for t in 
corpus.xml("tenant_catalog.xml", "tenant")}
+490|    allowances = {
+491|        key: int(value) for key, value in 
corpus.conf_sections("quota_limits.cfg")["override_allowance"].items()
+492|    }
+493|
+494|    applied, carried, groups = run_draw(rows, windows, instant, version, 
regions, allowances, aliases)
+495|    for row in applied:
+496|        row["_ms"] = ms_of(_source_row(row, groups))
+497|    amounts = pool_amounts(applied, windows)
+498|
+499|    corpus.note("clamp_rules.txt")
+500|    multipliers = {row["tenant"]: row for row in 
corpus.xlsx("tenant_multiplier_table.xlsx")}
+501|    corpus.note("risk_weight_rounding.txt")
+502|    tiers = {int(row["tier"]): int(row["base_threshold"]) for row in 
corpus.parquet("threshold_tier_curve.parquet")}
+503|
+504|    def compute(principal, tenant_id, level):
+505|        row_base = base_rows[principal]
+506|        mult = multipliers[tenant_id]
+507|        mult_bps = int(mult["mult_bps"])
+508|        cap = int(mult["max_records_cap"])
+509|        mine = sorted((r for r in applied if r["_principal"] == principal),
key=lambda r: r["seq"])
+510|        carry = sorted((r for r in carried if r["_principal"] == 
principal), key=lambda r: r["seq"])
+511|        sources = [_source_row(row, groups) for row in mine]
+512|
+513|        after_delta = int(row_base["max_records_base"])
+514|        after_delta += sum(int(src["max_records_delta"]) for src in 
sources)
+515|        after_delta += sum(int(r["max_records_delta"]) for r in carry if 
r["carry"] == "max_records_delta")
+516|        max_records = min(cap, scale_half_up(after_delta, mult_bps))
+517|
+518|        retry_limit = int(row_base["retry_limit_base"]) + 
sum(int(src["retry_limit_delta"]) for src in sources)
+519|
+520|        timeout_ms = int(row_base["timeout_seconds_base"]) * MS_PER_SECOND
+521|        for row in mine:
+522|            timeout_ms += amounts[row["seq"]]
+523|            timeout_ms = max(timeout_ms, TIMEOUT_FLOOR_MS)
+524|
+525|        terms = [int(row_base["risk_weight_base"])]
+526|        terms.extend(int(src["risk_delta"]) for src in sources)
+527|        terms.extend(int(r["risk_delta"]) for r in carry if r["carry"] == 
"risk_delta")
+528|        contribution = sum(scale_half_up(term, mult_bps) for term in terms)
+529|
+530|        return {
+531|            "max_records": max_records,
+532|            "retry_limit": retry_limit,
+533|            "timeout_ms": timeout_ms,
+534|            "contribution": contribution,
+535|            "base_threshold": tiers[level],
+536|            "applied": [row["override_id"] for row in mine],
+537|        }
+538|
+539|    spec = corpus.kv_text("convergence_spec.txt")
+540|    epsilon = int(spec["convergence_epsilon"])
+541|    max_iterations = int(spec["max_iterations"])
+542|    fallback = 
int(corpus.conf_sections("reconciliation_rules.conf")["reconcile"]["iteration_di
visor"])
+543|
+544|    reports = ledger_doc.get("reports") or []
+545|    resets = [int(r["seq"]) for r in reports if r.get("kind") == "reset"]
+546|    standing = [
+547|        r for r in reports if r.get("kind") == "curve" and (not resets or 
int(r["seq"]) > max(resets))
+548|    ]
+549|    for report in reports:
+550|        if report.get("kind") == "curve" and report not in standing:
+551|            rejected.append(
+552|                {
+553|                    "id": "curve report seq {0}".format(report["seq"]),
+554|                    "reason": "withdrawn by the reset at seq {0} in 
override_deltas.json".format(max(resets)),
+555|                }
+556|            )
+557|    if standing:
+558|        targets = []
+559|        for report in standing:
+560|            role = report["role"]
+561|            numbers = compute(
+562|                index[role + ".param_key"], index[role + ".tenant"], 
int(index[role + ".role_level"])
+563|            )
+564|            targets.append((numbers["base_threshold"], 
numbers["contribution"], int(report["converged"])))
+565|        divisors = [
+566|            candidate
+567|            for candidate in DIVISOR_RANGE
+568|            if all(
+569|                reconcile(bt, contrib, candidate, epsilon, 
max_iterations)[1] == want
+570|                for bt, contrib, want in targets
+571|            )
+572|        ]
+573|        divisor = divisors[0] if len(divisors) == 1 else None
+574|    else:
+575|        divisor = fallback
+576|
+577|    numbers = compute(param_key, tenant, role_level)
+578|    if divisor is None:
+579|        iterations = [numbers["base_threshold"]]
+580|        converged = None
+581|    else:
+582|        iterations, converged = reconcile(
+583|            numbers["base_threshold"], numbers["contribution"], divisor, 
epsilon, max_iterations
+584|        )
+585|
+586|    schema = corpus.json(manifest["schema_ref"])
+587|    defaults = corpus.json(manifest["trailer_ref"])
+588|    resolved = {
+589|        "profile": schema["profile"],
+590|        "producer": schema["producer"],
+591|        "container": schema["container"],
+592|        "checksum": defaults["checksum"],
+593|        "mode": defaults["mode"],
+594|        "max_records": numbers["max_records"],
+595|        "retry_limit": numbers["retry_limit"],
+596|        "timeout_ms": numbers["timeout_ms"],
+597|        "trailer": str(defaults["base_config"]["trailer"]),
+598|        "param_key": param_key,
+599|        "tenant": tenant,
+600|        "effective_version": version,
+601|        "risk_threshold": converged,
+602|    }
+603|
+604|    claimed = 
corpus.json("request_corpus.json")["entries"][0]["claimed_version"]
+605|    rejected.append(
+606|        {
+607|            "id": "claimed_version " + str(claimed),
+608|            "reason": "client-claimed version in request_corpus.json is not
authoritative;"
+609|            " the effective version comes from policy_version_map.json",
+610|        }
+611|    )
+612|    trace = {
+613|        "active_bundle": active_bundle,
+614|        "effective_version": version,
+615|        "param_key": param_key,
+616|        "tenant": tenant,
+617|        "client_version_ignored": True,
+618|        "applied_overrides": numbers["applied"],
+619|        "ledger_controls": ledger_controls,
+620|        "resolution_order": list(corpus.order),
+621|        "rejected_branches": rejected,
+622|    }
+623|    curve = {
+624|        "tier": role_level,
+625|        "base_threshold": numbers["base_threshold"],
+626|        "contribution": numbers["contribution"],
+627|        "iterations": iterations,
+628|        "converged": converged,
+629|    }
+630|    repair = {
+631|        name: find_repair(stored.get(name, []), 
ledger_doc["controls"][name], ms_of)
+632|        for name, status in ledger_controls.items()
+633|        if status == "mismatch"
+634|    }
+635|    return resolved, trace, curve, repair
+636|
+637|
+638|def cmd_init(args):
+639|    schema = load_json(args.schema)
+640|    data = {
+641|        "profile": schema["profile"],
+642|        "producer": schema["producer"],
+643|        "container": schema["container"],
+644|        "checksum": schema["checksum"],
+645|        "mode": "strict-local",
+646|    }
+647|    write_json(args.out, data)
+648|    print("initialized")
+649|
+650|
+651|def cmd_resolve(args):
+652|    resolved, trace, curve, repair = resolve_policy(args.data_dir)
+653|    out = Path(args.out)
+654|    write_json(out / "policy_resolved.json", resolved)
+655|    write_json(out / "dependency_trace.json", trace)
+656|    write_json(out / "threshold_curve.json", curve)
+657|    write_json(out / "ledger_repair.json", repair)
+658|    print(json.dumps(resolved, sort_keys=True))
+659|
+660|
+661|def cmd_set(args):
+662|    data = load_json(args.config)
+663|    data[args.key] = coerce(args.value)
+664|    assert_flat(data)
+665|    write_json(args.config, data)
+666|    print("set " + args.key)
+667|
+668|
+669|def cmd_validate(args):
+670|    schema = load_json(args.schema)
+671|    data = load_json(args.config)
+672|    assert_flat(data)
+673|    required = schema["required_keys"]
+674|    if isinstance(required, str):
+675|        required = [key.strip() for key in required.split(",") if 
key.strip()]
+676|    missing = [key for key in required if key not in data]
+677|    if missing:
+678|        raise SystemExit("missing: " + ",".join(missing))
+679|    print("ok")
+680|
+681|
+682|def cmd_report(args):
+683|    data = load_json(args.config)
+684|    commands = json.loads(args.commands)
+685|    report = {
+686|        "ok": True,
+687|        "tool": "sentinel-ctl",
+688|        "config_sha256": args.digest,
+689|        "commands": commands,
+690|        "trailer": data.get("trailer"),
+691|    }
+692|    write_json(args.out, report)
+693|    print("reported")
+694|
+695|
+696|def main():
+697|    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="sentinel-ctl")
+698|    sub = parser.add_subparsers(dest="command")
+699|
+700|    p = sub.add_parser("init")
+701|    p.add_argument("--schema", required=True)
+702|    p.add_argument("--out", required=True)
+703|    p.set_defaults(func=cmd_init)
+704|
+705|    p = sub.add_parser("resolve")
+706|    p.add_argument("--data-dir", required=True)
+707|    p.add_argument("--out", required=True)
+708|    p.set_defaults(func=cmd_resolve)
+709|
+710|    p = sub.add_parser("set")

into /app/out so they're visible alongside the required artifacts even though 
the summary only lists the seven main files. For report.json, I'll capture argv,
returncode, and stdout for each command that builds config.json (resolve, init, 
sets, validate) plus profile runs, excluding the export-report command itself. 
For tool.zip, I need it deterministic with sorted members, fixed 1980 
timestamps, consistent file mode, and matching compression type to the original.

Action: FileEditorAction

Arguments:
  command: "create"
  path: "/app/build.py"
  file_text: 
    """Drive sentinel-ctl to produce the delivered policy bundle artifacts."""
    
    from __future__ import annotations
    
    import hashlib
    import json
    import subprocess
    import sys
    import zipfile
    from pathlib import Path
    
    DATA = Path("/app/data")
    TOOL = Path("/app/tool")
    OUT = Path("/app/out")
    CLI = TOOL / "cli.py"
    SCHEMA = DATA / "config_schema.json"
    
    commands = []
    
    
    def run(*argv):
        proc = subprocess.run(
            [sys.executable, str(CLI), *argv], capture_output=True, text=True, 
check=True
        )
        commands.append(
            {
                "argv": ["sentinel-ctl", *argv],
                "returncode": proc.returncode,
                "stdout": proc.stdout.strip(),
            }
        )
        return proc.stdout
    
    
    def load(path):
        return json.loads(Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    
    
    def dump(path, data):
        Path(path).write_text(json.dumps(data, sort_keys=True, indent=2) + "\n",
encoding="utf-8")
    
    
    def build_config(target, values):
        run("init", "--schema", str(SCHEMA), "--out", str(target))
        for key in ("profile", "mode", "max_records", "retry_limit", 
"timeout_ms", "trailer"):
            run("set", "--config", str(target), "--key", key, "--value", 
str(values[key]))
        run("validate", "--schema", str(SCHEMA), "--config", str(target))
    
    
    def write_tool_zip(target, source_dir):
        with zipfile.ZipFile(target, "w") as zf:
            for name in sorted(p.name for p in source_dir.iterdir() if 
p.is_file()):
                info = zipfile.ZipInfo(name, date_time=(1980, 1, 1, 0, 0, 0))
                info.compress_type = zipfile.ZIP_STORED
                info.create_system = 3
                info.external_attr = 0o644 << 16
                zf.writestr(info, (source_dir / name).read_bytes())
    
    
    def main():
        OUT.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        run("resolve", "--data-dir", str(DATA), "--out", str(OUT))
    
        policy = load(OUT / "policy_resolved.json")
        curve = load(OUT / "threshold_curve.json")
        trace = load(OUT / "dependency_trace.json")
        repair = load(OUT / "ledger_repair.json")
    
        schema = load(SCHEMA)
        rules = load(DATA / "validation_rules.json")
        env = load(DATA / "env_overrides.json")
        format_tag = schema["profile"]
        config_keys = list(schema["required_keys"])
        trailer = policy["trailer"]
    
        build_config(OUT / "config.json", policy)
        config = load(OUT / "config.json")
    
        profiles = {}
        for name in rules["profiles"]:
            sidecar = load(DATA / ("sample_config_%s.json" % name))
            deltas = env[name]
            values = {
                "profile": "%s-%s" % (format_tag, name),
                "mode": deltas["mode"],
                "max_records": policy["max_records"] + 
sidecar["max_records_delta"],
                "retry_limit": policy["retry_limit"] + 
deltas["retry_limit_delta"],
                "timeout_ms": policy["timeout_ms"] + deltas["timeout_ms_delta"],
                "trailer": "%s:%s" % (trailer, name),
            }
            target = OUT / ("profile_%s.json" % name)
            build_config(target, values)
            profiles[name] = load(target)
    
        digest = hashlib.sha256((OUT / "config.json").read_bytes()).hexdigest()
        run(
            "export-report",
            "--config",
            str(OUT / "config.json"),
            "--out",
            str(OUT / "report.json"),
            "--digest",
            digest,
            "--commands",
            json.dumps(commands, sort_keys=True),
        )
    
        write_tool_zip(OUT / "tool.zip", TOOL)
    
        artifacts = list(rules["required_artifacts"])
        inputs = sorted(p.name for p in DATA.iterdir() if p.is_file())
        dump(
            OUT / "summary.json",
            {
                "artifact_count": len(artifacts),
                "artifacts": artifacts,
                "profiles": list(rules["profiles"]),
                "format_tag": format_tag,
                "trailer": trailer,
                "input_count": len(inputs),
                "inputs": inputs,
            },
        )
    
        present = all((OUT / name).exists() for name in artifacts)
        matches = config == {key: policy[key] for key in config_keys}
        flat = all(not isinstance(value, (dict, list)) for value in 
config.values())
        failures = [entry["argv"] for entry in commands if entry["returncode"] 
!= 0]
        dump(
            OUT / "validation_report.json",
            {
                "ok": present and matches and flat and not failures,
                "checks": {
                    "all_artifacts_present": present,
                    "config_matches_policy": matches,
                    "config_scalar_flat": flat,
                    "command_failures": failures,
                    "required_artifacts": artifacts,
                    "profiles": {
                        name: {"trailer": body["trailer"]} for name, body in 
profiles.items()
                    },
                },
                "policy_resolved": policy,
                "threshold_curve": curve,
                "dependency_trace": trace,
                "ledger_repair": repair,
            },
        )
        print("built", len(artifacts), "artifacts")
    
    
    if __name__ == "__main__":
        main()
    

  kind: "FileEditorAction"

Tokens: ↑ input 88.15K (total 1.16M) • cache hit 97.85% (total 92.37%) •  
reasoning 1.01K (total 14.43K) • ↓ output 3.14K (total 43.1K) • $ (total 2.1627)

Applying seq22 (OV-S, -900), seq23 (OV-R borrowing -900), and seq24 (OV-T, -800)
in ascending order against the 2000ms timeout with a 1000ms floor, I get 
2000→1100→200 (floored to 1000)→200 (floored to 1000), landing at a final 
timeout of 1000. Now I'm computing max_records starting from the base of 300.

For max_records, if I use the raw mrd values I hit the floor of 285 again, so I 
adjust OV-S and OV-T's mrd to -60 each to avoid capping: 300-60-5-60=175, scaled
by 1.075 and rounded gives 188, which is below the 285 cap so min(285,188)=188. 
For retry, I add up 2+0+1+0=3. For risk, I combine 57+0+3(scaled to 3)+0=64. 
With base_threshold 40 and d=10, I get t1=50 and t2=51.

For t3, 40+floor(115/10)=51, so the final threshold is 51. The applied_overrides
sequence runs ascending: OV-S at seq 22, OV-R at 23, OV-T at 24.

I also want to test an alias span that doesn't contain the instant timestamp, 
which should produce principal None but still consume an admit unit—though 
placing it at seq25 means admits are already exhausted by seq24, so it'd be 
refused regardless. Instead I should place that alias row earlier, around seq19,
so it consumes an admit unit and demonstrates the K042 rows later hitting the 
admit limit. Let me set up a separate simpler test case for this.

Placing OV-U with KOUT active at seq19, with WIN-AUTH going to 0 and an alias 
span that excludes the timestamp: it draws and gets applied, consuming an admit 
unit (3→2) and a region unit (5→4), but contributes to no principal since the 
alias doesn't match. Then seq22 and seq23 apply normally, dropping admit to 0, 
and seq24 gets refused.

values: timeout nets out to 1000, max_records computes through a scaling formula
to 253, retry sums to 3, and risk accumulates to 64, with convergence landing at
51. I'm double-checking that OV-U's zero delta doesn't skew the timeout pool 
since its target is 0. Now I'm counting the t-atlas control rows, landing on 6.

highest-seq row gets reinstated can't be spare. So the only spare row is seq21 —
removing it should drop row count to 5, mrd to 652, and ms to -2100, meaning the
expected repair output is [21]. Now I'll actually run this scenario to verify.

import json,hashlib,re,pathlib,zipfile
forb={'timestamp','generated_at','uuid','random_seed'}
def walk(o,path=''):
    if isinstance(o,dict):
        for k,v in o.items():
            assert k not in forb, (path,k)
            walk(v,path+'/'+k)
    elif isinstance(o,list):
        for i,v in enumerate(o): walk(v,path)
for p in sorted(pathlib.Path('.').glob('*.json')):
    d=json.loads(p.read_text()); walk(d)
    txt=p.read_text()
    assert len(p.read_bytes())<1048576, p
    hosts=re.findall(r'https?://|\b[\w.-]+\.(?:com|net|org|io|local)\b', txt)
    secrets=re.findall(r'(?i)(password|secret|api[_-]?key|token|BEGIN [A-Z 
]*PRIVATE KEY)', txt)
    print(p.name, len(p.read_bytes()), 'hosts',hosts,'secrets',secrets)
r=json.load(open('report.json'))
assert 
r['config_sha256']==hashlib.sha256(pathlib.Path('config.json').read_bytes()).hex
digest()

## What was wrong

The tool crashed on Python 3.12 (`configparser.SafeConfigParser`, removed in 
3.12) and, once running, compiled numbers from the wrong branches and the wrong 
arithmetic. Against the reference rules I found and fixed thirteen defects in 
`/app/tool/cli.py`:

| Defect | Authoritative rule |
|---|---|
| `SafeConfigParser` | `RawConfigParser` (3.12) |
| used `latest_bundle_id` (B9, *superseded*) | `stale_version_rules.txt`: prefer
`active_bundle_id` → **B7 / operator** |
| version from `request_corpus.json` `claimed_version` (v205) | 
`policy_version_map.json` selection → **v100** |
| closed window interval, no version check | `glossary.txt` half-open + 
`override_precedence.conf` `match_version` → `WIN-PRE` (end-exclusive) and 
`WIN-AUTH-V099` (v099) rejected |
| first matching override only | full ascending-`seq` draw with window `admit`, 
region `override_allowance`, `requires`, 
reissue/`reinstated`/`carry`/`withdrawn` |
| `min(cap, after_delta) * bps // 1000` | `clamp_rules.txt`: cap applied 
**after** half-up bps scaling |
| floor instead of half-up | `glossary.txt` `half_up` |
| timeout left in seconds | `units_reference.txt`: ms = s × 1000, pre-cutover 
(1650000000) deltas stored in **seconds**, window `timeout_pool` two-pass 
sharing, 1000 ms running floor |
| contribution floored once over the sum | `risk_weight_rounding.txt`: round 
**each term** separately |
| `tiers[role_level - 1]` | `convergence_spec.txt`: tier == `role_level` → 40 |
| single reconciliation step | iterate `t_next = base + 
floor((contribution+t)/divisor)` to `convergence_epsilon` |
| controls compared on raw deltas | controls are in **milliseconds** (this is 
what exposed the `t-atlas` ledger mismatch) |
| `required_keys` string iterated per character; unsorted/unindented JSON | 
handles list or CSV; canonical `sort_keys` + indent + trailing newline |

Every worked example in the reference material (requires-ordering,
